Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the logistic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 873 and the convergence rate is O(84/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the logistic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 873 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 84. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in logistic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 775 and the invariant I(A,B) = 395 mod 3.
